AA Prototype · Requirements catch-up

The catch-up: from the RFP to the catalogue

The prototype was built in July against the original RFP, in fourteen phases. The requirements catalogue has since become the source of truth and moved a long way: Bookings instead of Cards, Slots and Draft Lists, one Contract per Procedure locked at authorise, admin-decided hospital matching, an internal ledger with Xero as its mirror. This plan closes every verified gap in 37 further phases, numbered 14 to 44, each one focused session (at most two) with one coherent deliverable, and every automatic or external event given a demo button on the screen where it matters.

Catalogue commit 501b0b8 · gap analysis 2026-10-01 · plan rendered from plan.json and gaps.json

What changed since the prototype

The prototype is a sound shell on the old RFP model; the catalogue has moved the model underneath it. Of the roughly 330 graded items across EP-01 to EP-15, most gaps are structural, not cosmetic. About 15 "Matches" are all in places the catalogue did not change (canvas generation, tiered time rule, Xero pair and webhook, audit viewer, AUTHORISED lock, NHI lookup stub). The rest cluster in four places: the schedule (Slot/List/Draft List), the Contract-replaces-route pricing model, the prepayment and money flow, and a missing warning model. Several rulings the prototype built as hard rules are now reversed: prepayment blocks completion, AA fee is a 5% deduction, cover is requested and accepted. EP-04, EP-06 and EP-01 are the heaviest (XL, XL, L). Estimate: the first four themes below are most of the work. A reverse check (things the prototype has that the catalogue no longer wants) was not supplied to this summary; the removal list below is derived from the item gradings and DM deltas and should be confirmed against the RV- list when it exists.

The themes below are the gap analysis's own grouping. Each lists the phases that carry its items. Full reasoning: GAP-ANALYSIS.md.

  1. Schedule rebuilt on Slot, List and Draft List

    Split Slot from List, add Draft Lists, make availability a Slot status from one calendar, fix office-booked Free slots (P5). XL. OQ-64 open.

    19 items · built in phases 28, 29, 30, 31

  2. Anaesthetist moves own List; doer's List and payable

    Replace cover request/accept with immediate move to office or colleague's free Slot; Booking done by another moves to their List with payee following. L.

    8 items · built in phases 17, 32, 41

  3. Contract replaces the billing route

    Exactly-one Contract per Procedure, procedure-first filtered picker, versions, dated price lines, payment setting, billable party independent of pricing. XL.

    32 items · built in phases 18, 19, 20, 21, 22, 23, 24, 25, 42

  4. Multi-procedure rule and explicit primary Procedure

    Booking-level modifier split above four units with remainder to primary, isPrimary and set-primary, Copy as skeleton. M.

    7 items · built in phases 15, 23

  5. Prepayment reversed

    Prepaid RVG set trigger, automatic full estimate, approve-then-send with letter, no completion block, trust account, refund on cancel, repoint on move. XL.

    20 items · built in phases 26, 27, 41

  6. Warnings, never blocks

    One warning routine and record, admin to-do, triangles in all apps, submit confirm; convert gates to warnings. L; build early.

    9 items · built in phases 15a, 19, 21, 40

  7. Money model: ledger, AA fee, corrections

    Remove 5% deduction, monthly fee invoice, ledger receivable/payable pair, credit/additional/negative invoices, netting and remittance, BCTI, Xero-side payables paid. XL over several phases.

    24 items · built in phases 16, 39a, 22, 25, 36, 37, 39

  8. Events replace post-op addendum; look-back and search

    Pre/post-op events on a Procedure with own date, time or fixed fee, billable flag, office approval; billed Lists reachable; search by NHI or name. L; OQ-63 provisional.

    11 items · built in phases 39b, 40, 38a, 38, 39

  9. Intake becomes staged matching

    Imported rows staged with admin decisions, unmatched queue, per-hospital sync state; stop auto-apply; explicit save with change set and mailto update email. L.

    9 items · built in phases 33, 34, 35, 40a

  10. Patients and NHI

    Admin patient screen, add-NHI, missing-NHI problem list, authorise guard, age-based balance alert. M.

    6 items · built in phase 40

  11. Master data, profiles and reports

    Surgeon profile, rooms, blacklist, hospital emails, editable statuses, holiday calendar, anaesthetist bank/GST/start date, flat outstanding list without ageing, payout-basis GST, role-gated capture context and help. M to L, many small items.

    13 items · built in phases 17, 26, 29, 38, 42, 43a

How this works

  1. Get the owner decisions first. D1 to D11 (the table at the top of ROADMAP.md) each gate one phase. A phase whose answer has not come in builds the stated default and labels it provisional.
  2. Pick the next phase from the table below, respecting the order in "Sequencing and rules". The Contracts track (17 to 25) and the Schedule track (28 to 32) each run strictly in order.
  3. Open a fresh Claude Code session and paste that phase's kick-off prompt. The agent reads the roadmap, its phase doc, the gap detail for its items, the prototype maps and PROGRESS.md before touching anything.
  4. Drift check first. The catalogue keeps moving. Each session diffs its items against 501b0b8, reports what changed, and adapts the plan before building.
  5. The agent enters plan mode, turns the phase doc into work-sized steps, and waits for approval before changing any file.
  6. Every phase ends the same way: npm run build, npm run build:pwa, npx vitest run and (for UI work) npm run shots green; the checklist run and reported item by item; the adversarial review-and-fix pass (PROGRESS convention 18); the demo guide patched for any beat it broke; the catalogue screenshots brought in step with the app (the phase's capture recipes created or updated, then a full npm run capture in requirements-board/ with no failed recipe and npm run verify:board green, PROGRESS convention 19); a PROGRESS.md entry. Agents never commit.
  7. When the catalogue changes, follow README.md: re-grade the changed items, merge them into gaps.json, update the affected phase docs, and re-render this page with node docs/prototype-build/catch-up/tools/build-index.mjs.

Demo buttons live on their screens. Anything that cannot be shown through normal use (scheduled jobs, backend events, external systems) gets a trigger in the harness bar's "Demo actions" menu, listed only on the screen where it matters. The installed PWA has no harness bar, so it gets the same list as a badged demo-actions sheet, plus office stand-ins for beats that wait on the office. Phase 14 builds the mechanism; every later phase registers into it.

The checklists are the contract. Each phase ends with a short manual test checklist. The kick-off prompt makes the agent run and report it, but click through it yourself too: it doubles as a rehearsal for the workshop beats that phase touches.

Sequencing and rules

Owner decisions. D1 to D8 and D10 were answered at the 2026-10-01 meeting with Greg (table at the top of ROADMAP.md) and their phases build the answer, not a default: D1 monthly AA fee invoice from settings (16), D2 insurer and funding source on neither Booking nor Patient (20), D3 any base units with an after-procedure office warning (19), D4 child billable party a mild warning (21), D5 no prepayment gate, a warning in both apps (15a, 27), D6 prepayment invoice generated at setup and sent on admin approval (27), D7 anaesthetist moves their own List with no confirmation (32), D8 flat outstanding list (38), D10 free-form additional invoice (39). D9 billed Lists (38) and D11 Booking without NHI (40) are still open: build the default, label it provisional, and confirm at the drift check. Open questions with a recommendation are built as that recommendation, labelled provisional and kept in one place (the meeting's working rule).

Order. Run 14 and 15 first, in either order; 15 before any phase that edits booking code (if 14 runs first, 15's rename covers the registry's patterns and labels). 15a (warnings) runs after both and before 19, 21, 27 and 40, which register warning rules; it removes the prepayment completion gate. 16 runs straight after 15a, so the first milestone (after 16) includes the warnings, with fee removal re-greened as step 1 before the monthly fee invoice. The Contracts track runs strictly in order, 17 to 25: 18 keeps today's resolver; 19 (RVG and procedure masters, procedure-first picker) comes straight after 18 so base-unit sources are final before 20's Contract picker, 23's Booking-level engine and 25's lock; 20 leaves two interims (payer is the Contract holder until 21 stores a billable party with its override; funderOverride stays the split until 22's payment setting); 23 builds the combination Contracts 39 needs; 25 locks what 18 to 24 built, including 24's adjustment and the payee. 26 and 27 run back to back after 25; 20's office-set prepayment flag is the interim until 27. The Schedule track runs strictly in order, 28 to 32; 31 and 32 reuse 17's blacklist helper; 32 also needs 31 (a List moved to the office becomes a Draft List) and 27 (a move re-checks the prepayment). Intake 33 to 35 runs after 31 and 20; 34 also needs 27, which adds the estimated duration per Procedure (DM-40) that 34's PDF review edits; 35 also needs 25 and 32. 36 runs after 16, 22, 25 and 27; then 37, 38 and 39 in any order; 39a after 37 and 39; 38a after 38; 39b after 38a and 39, and soon after 39, which withdraws the anaesthetist's post-op Card flow. 40 after 15a, 34 and 36; 40a after 26 and 40; 41 after 27, 39 and 39a; 42 after every master-data phase; 43 after 42; 43a after 21, 24, 31 and 39b; 44 last. Contracts and Schedule are independent from 17 until 32; Contracts go first because they are mostly Confirmed and carry the S3 to S5 money beats.

BCTI granularity (provisional, one place). The catalogue says both 'one BCTI per procedure' (US-09.1.4 note, OQ-29, note #41) and 'the same value as its receivable' (OQ-42), and a receivable can hold several Procedures, so both cannot hold (DM-22 says so). The plan builds one BCTI, the ACCPAY, per receivable invoice, the same value: the transcript's 'per transaction' (B L71-81), which US-10.2.1's release needs. 16 counts BCTIs for the fee run through one pure, tested function; 22 (Split gives two invoices, so two BCTIs), 36 (the ledger's payable legs, with a parity test), 39 (additional invoices) and 39b (event invoices) feed it and keep it the only count. 'One per procedure' stays an open point to raise with AA's accountant beside OQ-29 and OQ-60; if it flips, the count function, the $700 seed and the S3 and S4 figures are re-baselined in one place.

Demo triggers. 14 re-homes every existing Control Panel trigger onto its screen and adds useDemoTriggerContext for state outside the URL; later phases register or re-point entries, never add to the Control Panel page, which becomes the index (scenario jumps, clock, reset). Entries declare routes, surfaces (bar, PWA, both), run(api, ctx) on the entity in the URL or the published context, and a disabled state; bodies live in src/shared or src/store for pwaPurity. 15a's 'Raise sample warnings' is shared: each phase that adds a warning rule adds its sample. PWA parity: every beat whose mobile side waits on the office, a colleague or a backend event gets a PWA-only stand-in on that mobile screen (listed per phase); 'Play the office' defaults OFF with a badge, replaced by 14's per-List 'Office authorises this List'; 44 audits parity beat by beat.

Demo guide. Each phase patches the beats it breaks in docs/demo-guide, the same sections of master-demo-guide.html and the Control Panel scenario text, in the same session. Milestone phases (16, 25, 27, 32, 35, 39a) end with a consistency read of the master guide. 14 adds an interim Future-scope caveat to S1; 15a turns S4 Beat 1's gate into a warning; 34 rebuilds S1; 44 rewrites S1 to S5 and regenerates the master guide in full.

Every phase opens with a drift check against 501b0b8 and closes with npm run build, npm run build:pwa, npx vitest run, and npm run shots (Playwright) for any phase touching UI, all green; the adversarial review pass (PROGRESS convention 18); PERSIST_VERSION bumped on seed changes; Decisions-log entries where a July ruling is superseded (route model, 5% fee netting, Copy as an additional procedure, addendum Card, prepayment completion gate, silent BTM fallback, cover-request flow, hospital auto-apply); a PROGRESS entry.

Placement notes for the 2026-10-01 update. 15a is new and early: the warning routine is cheap over today's data and four later phases plug rules into it. US-05.5.2 moves from 19 and US-03.3.6 from 39 to 39b, because the ACC pre-op assessment and late lines are now pre-op and post-op events; 39b carries all of US-03.3.6 (a date per line, preset line types, Contract add-on fee lines from 18's add-on schedule lines). US-08.6.4 is unparked into 39 (OQ-53 answered; its combination Contract is 23's). 39a is split from 39 so neither passes two sessions: 39 raises the negative invoice, 39a nets it, and 39a builds OQ-71's recommendation in full (carry forward, then invoice the anaesthetist after a set period through 16's anaesthetist invoice path), so it is estimated at 2 sessions. FT-06.4 and US-06.4.2 stay in 41: OQ-03 made them simpler (no credit), not more urgent. US-09.3.1 goes to 16, which already edits the Xero simulator. US-15.0.1 gets its own late phase (43a) once capture screens settle; 20 builds the anaesthetist picker to its rule. 32 becomes the anaesthetist's own move and takes US-01.4.6; the moved prepaid Booking's payee repoint (US-06.5.4) is 41's. Critic points not followed (first plan): the escalating unpaid-prepayment warning (US-06.3.2) and the re-check (US-06.3.5) stay in 27, not 41, so S4 Beat 1 keeps a control; US-04.2.2 closes in 24, not 23, because its last missing part is the allows-adjustment rule. Critic point not followed (second review): Phase 32 keeps its file name and slug (phase-32-swap-requests), because the plan tools pin an existing phase's doc path in plan.json and a slug-only rename would put slug, doc and links out of step; the title and all app-facing copy already say 'move', and renaming the two files is a separate mechanical step if wanted. Phase 16 is estimated at 2 sessions; the plan totals about 63 sessions.

The phases

ContradictsPartialMissingMatchesGroupingData model (DM)Reverse check (RV)
14

Screen-contextual demo triggers

Foundations Start here 2 sessions plan doc ↗ 1 item covered

Build one shared, pure trigger registry in src/shared/demoTriggers (its own index, modelled on demoClockShortcuts, not re-exported from the shared component barrel), plus a small useDemoTriggerContext hook for screen state the URL cannot carry, such as the Admin Integrations tab and the simulator's selected message. Each entry declares its route patterns, where it shows (harness bar, PWA sheet or both), a run(api, ctx) that acts on the entity in the URL or on the published state, a disabled reason, and an index link with an optional hint. The harness bar gets one 'Demo actions' pill that lists only the current screen's triggers and is hidden when there are none. The PWA gets the same list as a badged bottom sheet. Every Control Panel trigger moves to its own screen, except 'Run payables', whose home is the Billing monitor's existing product button. The Control Panel becomes the index, keeping scenario jumps, clock and reset. FT-13.5 gets a 'Simulate sign-in attempts' trigger on Admin Audit that writes five audit rows. 'Play the office' is off by default, badged when on, and replaced in the story by a per-List 'Office authorises this List'. The HL7/FHIR tooling gets interim Future-scope badges, and S1 gets a caveat.

FT-13.5board
Demo triggers and risks
  • Admin Audit: 'Simulate sign-in attempts' (an Admin sign-in with MFA, a failed attempt, an anaesthetist self-service password reset)
  • Admin Billing monitor: 'Trigger billing failure', 'Arm handoff failure', 'Run payables', 'Run reconciliation poll', 'Run archive job' (re-homed from the Control Panel)
  • Admin Review, on Dr Sharma's Tue 14 Jul List: 'Stage post-op scenario' (re-homed)
  • Admin Integrations, Surgeon PDFs tab (read through the context hook): 'Ingest PDF row' (re-homed)
  • Admin invoice document and Xero pair detail: 'Payment received (webhook)' Full or Half, and 'Replay last event', for the invoice in the URL (re-homed)
  • Xero sim: 'Run reconciliation poll', 'Run archive job' (re-homed)
  • Integrations simulator, Admin Integrations and mobile Lists: 'Fire hospital message' and 'Replay last (dedupe)', badged Future scope (re-homed; S1 fires it from the mobile screen)
  • PWA sheet, mobile List: 'Office authorises this List' (so 'Play the office' can default OFF, badged when on)
  • PWA sheet, mobile Balances: 'Payment received' Full or Half on an open invoice

24

Contract-gated anaesthetist adjustment

Contracts After phase 23 1 session plan doc ↗ 7 items covered

Gives the anaesthetist an adjustment as its own record, separate from the office override (today's priceOverride, renamed officeOverride). It is a percent discount (above 0, up to 100) or a fixed final price, never a dollar amount, and always needs a reason. It is offered only when the Procedure's Contract has the new allowsAnaesthetistAdjustment flag set and B, T and M are recorded (B and M read from the primary since Phase 23). It is applied after 23's Booking-level base and before the office override, which works on every Contract, including a fixed-fee one. Base units are never touched, even at 100%. The field is absent where the Contract does not allow it, and the anaesthetist sees no fee. Review, the admin total and the invoice show both layers. This closes US-04.2.2.

27

Prepayment lifecycle

Prepayment After phases NaN, 25, 26 2 sessions plan doc ↗ 14 items covered

Prepayment comes from the anaesthetist's prepaid set, only when a person pays (OQ-73). A pure estimator works out base + time from estimated duration + contingency setting at the anaesthetist's own rate, and stores the full estimate on the Booking. The engine generates the invoice at setup and holds it until an admin approves and sends it (D6). Status tracks part-paid, the warning goes mild then strong in both apps and never blocks (D5). Re-checks run on change and move, and a move keeps the agreed amount (OQ-70). The balance invoice names the prepayment.

Open questions: OQ-38 · OQ-70 · OQ-73 · OQ-75

Demo triggers and risks
  • Admin Day: 'Move to 2 days before procedure' (a demo clock jump that turns the unpaid-prepayment warning from mild to strong)
  • Admin Day and Booking detail: 'Add prepaid-list Booking' (creates a Booking matching Dr Souter's prepaid set on her next open List, so its invoice appears 'Awaiting approval')
  • Booking detail and Admin Invoices: 'Approve and send' on a generated prepayment invoice (product)
  • Re-homed 'Payment received (webhook)' Half on the prepayment invoice shows part-paid
  • PWA sheet, mobile Booking: 'Office approves and sends the prepayment invoice'

Risks. The gate is already gone (15a); this phase adds derivation, the approval step and escalation. EP-06, US-06.2.1, US-06.2.2, US-06.3.1 and US-06.3.5 are Verify. The contingency buffer is OQ-38 (two units as a setting, shown in the estimate, not recorded as modifiers) and the RVG time-rule check is OQ-75. S4 Beat 1 (Riley, Nair) is rewritten here around generate, approve and send. This builds on billing cases that Phase 36 promotes. The estimated-duration field added here is the one Phase 34's PDF review edits. Letters, overpaid settlement, the trust hold, refunds and the moved-Booking payee are Phase 41.

28

Slot and List split

Schedule After phases 14, 15 2 sessions plan doc ↗ 8 items covered

Split the canvas into Slots and Lists. Every active anaesthetist gets an AM and a PM Slot each day from their start date, each with a fixed id, holding its availability status (free by default), a note and optional time overrides. Per OQ-17/OQ-27 (answered), the status on the Slot is the one mechanism, so nothing is reconciled. A List becomes its own record with a slotId and a kind. It is created only by office assignment (surgeon and hospital required) or by recurring-booking projection, and then shows in place of the status. One pure displayStatusKey(slot, list) gives the same status in all three apps and never reads Bookings. Default AM/PM times come from one settings record. Reassign moves a List between Slots intact, and the vacated Slot defaults to free. DRAFT now reads "Open" and PERSIST_VERSION is bumped. OQ-64 is built as recommended and labelled provisional in one place.

29

Availability calendar and status master

Schedule After phase 28 2 sessions plan doc ↗ 5 items covered

Anaesthetists keep their Slots' availability from a month calendar on mobile and web, weeks ahead. Slots are Free by default; they can mark days off ahead, add a repeating series, and change or delete one instance. The calendar writes Slot.status directly (one mechanism, OQ-27). A bookable status clears an earlier availability conflict. A closed status flags a List through one availabilityClash function, which Phase 31 replaces. Slot statuses become editable master data: Free, Holiday and Unavailable, coloured from the six design tokens and labelled provisional under OQ-64. The List types stay a fixed attribute.

While working:
- Mock backend only. Every write goes through a store action and the audited mutate(), with before/after metas and demo-clock timestamps. Components never own domain state. setAvailabilityRange, clearAvailability, createAvailabilitySeries and deleteAvailabilitySeries are each one mutate(), all or nothing on refusal, with one audit entry plus one entry per List affected.
- One mechanism (OQ-27 answered): the calendar writes the Slot's own status directly. A series is a rule (weekdays, sessions, every 1 to 4 weeks, start, optional until) plus exceptions; it writes its Slots, and a single-instance edit or delete is stored as an exception so no later series write or clock roll forward overwrites it. Nothing reads availability from anywhere but Slots. A series fills only Slots at the default status and in no other series, and reports the rest as left as they are. rollCanvasForward applies active series to new far-edge Slots after generation, leaving the generator's RNG untouched. Series rules live in src/domain/availabilitySeries.ts, pure and tested.
- The Slot status set becomes master data (masters.slotStatuses), availability values only (the OQ-17 answer: they belong to the Slot, not the List). Seed the design's three: Free (default, dashed), Holiday (long label "On holiday") and Unavailable (hatched), keyed by 28's stored values (free, holiday, unavailable), with today's labels and colours. No "available for emergency" (US-01.2.1 no longer lists it). The List types (private, public, pre-op) stay a fixed List attribute with their own tokens, and their keys are reserved so no status can take them. The values, names and colours are OQ-64: one provisional line in the Admin Slot statuses header, nowhere else.
- The only closed sets left are the palette (the six Design Language status tokens in src/theme/statusColours.ts, same hexes) and the List types. No new hex anywhere. The hatched and dashed treatments generalise with tokens only, and today's exact strings must still come out for the seeded statuses and every List.
- No literal availability keys outside src/domain/slotStatus.ts and the seed. Finders, filters, counts and clashes use isOpenForBooking, isClosed and isOpenSlot.
- Availability is never merged into the List. A List in a Slot shows in place of the status. The clash comes from one pure function, availabilityClash: a closed status over a List flags it once, replacing rather than stacking; a bookable status clears that List's availability conflict and raises nothing. The List's surgeon, hospital, Bookings, times and List type are never touched. Phase 31 later replaces the flag with a Draft List (the OQ-64 recommendation), so keep it in that one place.
- One display key space: the display key is list.kind when a List is in the Slot, otherwise slot.status unchanged, typed ListKind | SlotStatusKey. Draft Lists and the day dashboard

Schedule After phases 17, 30 2 sessions plan doc ↗ 10 items covered

Adds the Draft List, built as OQ-44 was answered: a List with no anaesthetist and no Slot. Its hospital, surgeon, day and session are all required, and it can hold Bookings before anyone is assigned. The office sees each Draft List flagged "Unassigned" with how long it has waited: on the Day view's right rail, in a band on the Day dashboard, on its own page and as an amber nav badge. Only the office assigns one, into the Slot its day and session fix. An occupied Slot is refused. Closed Slots and blacklisted pairings warn but still go ahead, and every step is audited. The office can also remove a Draft List or change its date. Provisionally (OQ-64), when an anaesthetist marks a Slot unavailable, their open Lists go back to the office as Draft Lists. This is one rule that replaces 29's availabilityClash 'flag', and the Conflicts screen still lists those Lists (US-01.5.4). The pairing rule is enforced everywhere: every List has one hospital and one surgeon, and AA rooms becomes a location. The Day dashboard shows each block's booking count.

While working:
- Mock backend only. Every write goes through a store action and the audited mutate(), with before and after metas and timestamps from the demo clock. Components never own domain state. The writes are create, assign, re-date and remove for Draft Lists, Bookings added to and moved off them through the existing Booking actions, the unavailability return, and the pairing refusals.
- A Draft List is a List record with no anaesthetist and no Slot, plus a draft trail (origin, source, since, by). There is no separate collection, so its Bookings hang off its List id and assignment keeps the id.
  - createDraftList must leave schedule.slots and every other List untouched; prove it with a test.
  - Only assignDraftList puts it in a Slot, through attachListToSlot, in the Slot its day and session fix, in one commit with a draftList.assign meta; the draft trail is kept with assignedAtISO. Then Phase 27's syncPrepayment (cause listMoved) and 15a's warning routine run for its Bookings. A Booking on a Draft List has no anaesthetist yet, so syncPrepayment must generate and withdraw nothing for it.
  - A List only becomes a Draft List through detachListToDraft (the unavailability route here; Phase 32's move to the office later).
  - removeDraftList soft-removes and refuses while it holds an active Booking; redateDraftList moves it and its Bookings to another day and keeps its waiting time.
  - A Draft List cannot be submitted, authorised or billed, and an anaesthetist cannot add a Booking to one.
  - No projection, roll-forward or new-anaesthetist path may overwrite a List id a Draft List holds, and a recurring booking never projects a second List for a day whose List was returned to the office (the Draft List keeps its recurringBookingId).
- Warn, never block, except for the real guards:
  - A closed Slot (Unavailable, Holiday or any closed status) or a blacklisted choice is selectable, warned before save, and assigned. A closed Slot gets a conflict through Phase 30's path, and a blacklisted pairing gets 17's acknowledgement entry.
  - Only these refuse: an occupied Slot, a missing hospital, surgeon, day or session, a passed day, removing a Draft List with active Bookings, and the office-only guard.
  - A Draft List at a hospital with a holiday carries Phase 30's holiday conflict like any List, and shows on the Conflicts screen as "Unassigned".
- The unavailability route (provisional, OQ-64): unavailabilityOutcome decides it and UNAVAILABLE_LISTS_BECOME_DRAFT_LISTS switches it. It lives in 29's domain/slotStatus.ts and replaces availabilityClash's 'flag' branch, so there is still one clash rule. Only open (DRAFT), today-or-later Lists convert, on every path that closes a Slot (setAvailability, setAvailabilityRange, createAvailabilitySeries and the series roll forward; Phase 30's Simulate sickness goes through setAvailability, and its choices skip Draft Lists). Submitted Lists keep Phase 30's conflict. The seed follows the same function. With the constant off, every Phase 30 test passes unchanged. US-01.5.4 AC1 still holds: Phase 30's Conflicts screen lists a returned List as a derived "Anaesthetist unavailable" row (Unassigned, was Dr X) until it is assigned or removed. The anaesthetist's confirm and result copy say the List goes back to the office, with a provisional badge, and never say "Draft List".
- One pairing rule. It lives in the pure pairingIssues helper, and every write path calls it. List.hospitalId and List.surgeonId become required, and List.anaesthetistId and slotId become optional (AssignedList and DraftList). The seed changes are:
  - the Fitzgerald Tue 21 PM "Surgeon TBC" List goes and her Slot is left empty; the seed gains three request Draft Lists (St George's with Mr S. Tan, Southern Cross with Ms K. Patel holding two Bookings, Christchurch Eye Surgery as the long wait);
  - Phase 30's seeded sickness (Dr Rutherford's Wed 22 AM Slot Unavailable, his Christchurch Eye Surgery List flagged) goes through the same rule, so that List becomes a Draft List from unavailability, Bookings kept;
  - the pre-op Lists sit at the new AA rooms location;
  - every pre-op and public List (all 16 null-surgeon recurring-booking templates plus the RNG acute branch) names a surgeon outside every Contract scope and the active blacklist;
  - every backdrop List gets a hospital.
  Remove every "AA rooms", "Not assigned" and "Surgeon TBC" fallback, and every "Unassigned" that is not the Draft List flag.
- Determinism first:
  - The acute surgeon comes from its own slotRng stream, so the fill stream and every Booking are unchanged.

Phase 28's "Office assigns a List to my next free Slot" stays beside it.
  Add Booking, Change date and Remove are product actions, not triggers.

  - each move writes exactly one list.ownerMove event (from, to, by, when, route) and no second list.reassign;
  - the receive rule exists once, and pushTargets uses it.
- The doer rule (US-01.4.6): moveBookingToDoer, for the List's owner or the office, moves a Booking onto the doer's List for the same day and session. If there is none, it creates one in the doer's Slot through placeListOnSlot; a closed Slot is flagged, not refused. It writes one booking.movedToDoer event. The payable follows through the List's owner and Phase 25's lock. Keep reassignBooking's notOwnList refusal everywhere else.
- Prepayment: every Booking moved by any of the three actions gets Phase 27's re-check (syncPrepayment in its plan; use the name delivered) once after commit, with causes listMoved and bookingMoved (Phase 41 keys on them), and so does a Draft List assignment. A sent prepayment keeps its agreed amount and the doer is paid (OQ-70 recommendation); a held one is withdrawn and regenerated by 27's rule. Repointing a raised prepayment's payable is Phase 41's.
- The blacklist warns and never blocks:
  - the anaesthetist sees only pairingPromptForAnaesthetist's message (ANAESTHETIST_PAIRING_PROMPT): no reason, no surgeon name, no entry id, no blacklist word;

The office sees a block on the Day band and a line in the SlotDrawer; the colleague sees a Moved to you panel and a List detail line. There is no notification entity, no queue and no badge.

The five other providers are daily sheets imported by hand, never labelled a feed. The sync pulls on a schedule, on opening the matching screen and on Sync now; each hospital shows its last successful sync and any failed attempt; a failed sync loses nothing and the next good sync catches up.
- Scheduled pulls run on the demo clock only: a clockAdvanced event and a wired job, no setInterval, no Date.now(), no new Date(), no Math.random(). A clock jump runs one catch-up pull. The on-open pull must be a no-op at the same demo instant so StrictMode does not double it.

Explicit save and the update email

Intake After phases 17, 25, 32, 33 2 sessions plan doc ↗ 6 items covered

Admin draft-then-save with change sets, as-at history and row-version merge or clash; an on-demand mailto update email (rooms for a Booking change, hospital for a cover change, including after an anaesthetist's own move); Add Booking on a booked List; anaesthetists keep immediate saves.

DM-35US-02.3.1boardUS-02.3.2boardUS-02.3.3boardUS-02.5.5boardUS-02.5.6board

Open questions: OQ-65 · OQ-69

Demo triggers and risks
  • Admin Booking detail while editing: 'Someone else saves this Booking now' (reads the edit draft through the context hook; a different-field merge, or a same-field clash on Save)
  • Admin Booking detail and the office's move notice: 'Draft update email' preview panel alongside the real mailto (product)
  • PWA sheet, mobile Booking: 'Office edits this Booking' (a saved office change arrives on the handset)

Risks. Because nothing applies silently, the concurrency trigger simulates a second office user, not the hospital feed. The shared Booking detail must keep the mobile and web immediate-save paths intact. Whether a List reassignment is itself a save is left open by the catalogue: treat it as its own event. S5 Beat 1 audit gains change sets.

What to check when it's done

OQ-69 and OQ-65 are still open; their recommendations are built behind one labelled constant each (UPDATE_EMAIL_OFFER, EMAIL_TRIGGERS) with provisional captions. D7 is reflected through Phase 32's moves. D2 (no insurer on the Booking) is reflected.

While working:
- Mock backend only. The save is one office-only store action through the audited mutate(); every write carries before/after metas and demo-clock timestamps. Components never own domain state.
- Draft-then-save is Admin only. Mobile, web and the PWA mount no draft provider and keep their immediate saves exactly as today; the anaesthetist never calls saveBookingChanges.
- Nothing on the Admin Booking detail may bypass the draft. Drafted edits go through the booking-store context; commands (complete, uncomplete, cancel, copy, move, and the prepayment or additional-invoice actions where built) act on the real store and are disabled while there are unsaved changes. A drafted action may touch only the snapshot's slices and never emit an app event (the emitter is shared by every store); a demo Reset discards a dirty draft. Add the source-scanning test that enforces it. In draft mode, sheet buttons read Apply, not Save.
- One save is one change set: one change-set id across the entries, a booking.save header, the office as the actor, and the row version up by one. rowVersion and the lastModified stamps never appear in audit before or after. A save runs 27's syncPrepayment once on the real store; in the draft store it is a no-op, so a draft never touches billing or emits an app event. A List reassignment is its own event with its own change set, not a save.
- Nothing is lost silently. A different-field concurrent change merges and the admin is told; a same-field change goes to the clash sheet; a cancel, move or authorise in between blocks the save with a plain reason. Merge against the real state at Save time. Remap draft-created ids against the real counters and re-check the existing invariants (22's split-share validation, one primary Procedure, Contract eligibility) on the merged result.
- Keep the change-set maths, the merge rules, the as-at reconstruction and the email builder pure, in src/domain, with Vitest tests. Order as-at by audit sequence, not time (the demo clock has minute resolution).
- The email is a real mailto link. The system sends nothing and never records an email as sent: opening a draft writes one "Update email drafted (not sent)" entry that moves the last-email mark, and nothing else. Per OQ-69's recommendation it is an on-demand button over the changes remembered since the last email, netted per field (UPDATE_EMAIL_OFFER, one labelled constant; the prompt-after-save value also works). The whole encoded link is at most 2,000 characters (one labelled constant), and a shortened body says so. Only the allowlisted fields appear: never an NHI, date of birth, internal notes, Contract, billable party or money. Recipients follow EMAIL_TRIGGERS as OQ-46 answered: the surgeon's rooms for a Booking change, the hospital contact for a cover change.
- The cover-change email is offered wherever a List changes hands: the reassign flow's last step (no more auto-close), 31's Draft List assignment when the List had an anaesthetist before, the List drawer, and 32's office notice when an anaesthetist moves their own List into a colleague's Slot (OQ-65, provisional). A move to the office offers nothing until the office assigns the Draft List. A doer move (moveBookingToDoer) changes no List's anaesthetist, so it is a Booking move for the rooms, not a cover change.
- Migrate the framed build's router to a data router for useBlocker, keeping every URL and Playwright entry point; leave pwa/main.tsx alone. Run npm run shots straight after the migration.
- Determinism: no Date.now(), new Date() or Math.random(). Seed rowVersion 1 in the seed builders, leave the canvas generator untouched, and bump PERSIST_VERSION by one.
- Saves that do not come from the Admin draft (the S5 jump and both demo triggers) go through the saveBookingPatch convenience over saveBookingChanges, never hand-built snapshots.
- Demo triggers go into Phase 14's registry, never the Control Panel page: "Someone else saves this Booking now" on the Admin Booking detail (bar, reads the published draft, saves as the fictional second office user Tama R. through the real save path) and "Office edits this Booking" on the mobile Booking (PWA only, office stand-in badge). Bodies live in src/shared so pwaPurity holds. "Draft update email" is a product action with a badged preview panel.
- Keep S2 intact: phone advice on a free Slot and its isScriptedS2Booking prefill still work, the Beat 3 reassignment now ends on the email step, and 32's own-move beat gains the office's email offer. Add Booking (the control 31 put on the Draft List drawer) also works on a booked List.
- Design: teal is the only action colour and crimson never appears on the new surfaces; warning tint for unsaved, success tint for saved, neutral for notices; Admin overlays through useSurface().Overlay; proper desktop layout. No en or em dashes in any app copy, email text or trigger label.

37

Xero mirror resilience

Money After phase 36 1 session plan doc ↗ 3 items covered

The Xero mirror now behaves like a real integration that can fail, and the Phase 36 ledger stays the system of record. An ACCPAY is paid in Xero's payables run, and the engine records the disbursement against the payable leg only when a bill-paid webhook or the poll reports it, dated at the bill payment. The Billing monitor's own "Run payables in Xero" button stays as the office view. In a simulated outage, every engine-to-Xero call waits in a visible queue with an attempt count and backoff on the demo clock. Authorising still works, and restoring Xero drains the queue with no duplicates. The armed handoff fault becomes one queued failure that retries by itself. An invoice voided or amended directly in Xero is flagged at the next poll, and the office records a provisional resolution that moves no money. A bulk St George's remittance pays two invoices through the normal webhook and leaves one unmatched line in a Xero-side Bank reconciliation tab. A source-scan test keeps remittance matching out of the engine.

EP-09boardUS-09.2.4boardUS-10.2.4board
Demo triggers and risks
  • Xero sim and Billing monitor: 'Simulate Xero outage' / 'Restore Xero'
  • Xero pair detail: 'Void this invoice in Xero'
  • Xero pair detail: 'Pay anaesthetist in Xero (payables run)'
  • Xero Invoices tab: 'Simulate bulk hospital remittance'

Risks. Backoff runs on the demo clock, not wall time. Bulk matching stays out of the engine by design; add a scope callout.

What to check when it's done
  • Outage on: authorising a List bills and queues one handoff per ledger pair (attempts, next try, backoff 2/4/8 min on the demo clock, alert after five). Restore drains in order, with exactly one Xero pair, contact, receipt and disbursement per event. Arm handoff failure retries by itself.
  • With a missed webhook, 'Pay anaesthetist in Xero' leaves the payable leg released but undisbursed until the poll, which records it once at the bill payment's date. Only recordDisbursement, via applyDisbursement, writes a LedgerDisbursement. The Phase 36 ledger stays in balance and the bank hold still bites.
  • Void or 'Edit this invoice's total in Xero' shows nothing in the engine until the poll, which flags it once. Resolve records a provisional outcome and changes no amount. A voided ACCREC takes no payment. The bulk remittance pays oa01 and oa03 by webhook and leaves one unmatched line in Xero. Mitchell's oa07 is untouched, and scopeFence.test.ts passes.

While working:
- Mock backend only, through store actions and the audited mutate(). The ledger (Phase 36) stays the system of record and the engine never performs a disbursement: the run is paid in Xero, and the engine records each bill payment (37's recordDisbursement) and each credit allocation (the new recordOffset) only when Xero reports it, by webhook, poll or the outage queue, idempotent by key.
- The approval gate is the point of US-10.2.6: no path pays a payable that is not an approved, unsettled run line (runPayables, disbursePayable, the Xero-side bill payment, the Xero pair trigger, a replay, the poll, the queue). The one-click demo payouts approve first so the gate has no back door: the Xero sim's "Simulate payment and payout" (S3 Beat 3) and the payout half of 39's "Stage refund after payout" go through approveAndPayPairForDemo (a one-pair staged run), and 36's PWA "Office runs payables" becomes "Office approves and runs payables". A run is frozen at approval; a stale run is superseded and re-approved, never edited.
- Netting is pure billing maths in src/domain/billing/payablesRun.ts with Vitest worked examples, including US-10.2.5's 19 positives and one negative. It nets each negative invoice's toNetAmount (what 39 left open), never its offsetAmount. Net per anaesthetist is never below zero; the remainder is carried forward, and a carry-only run is approvable. The offset is its own amount on the payable leg (offsetAmount), never counted as cash, and the whole ledger stays in balance after approval, sending, detection, a recovery invoice and its payment.
- The recovery invoice goes through Phase 16's raiseAnaesthetistInvoiceInto (generalised, not copied), as its own receivable-only 'recovery' ledger pair with no GST (provisional, one constant). It is never AA fee income, never a BCTI, never GST activity and never in a run. OQ-71's rule lives in one pure function and one store action over one setting.
- runExclusionFor is the only exclusion point. Today it excludes nothing: 26's bank details are display-only, so a missing account is flagged (missingBank, a null destination), never excluded; if 37 shipped a heldForBank refusal, it becomes the first reason. Netting reads every open negative whatever its credit note's cause.
- Time from the demo clock only, string date maths for periods and ages; no Date.now(), new Date() or Math.random(). The staging bodies are deterministic.
- Seeded disbursements become backdrop runs (one per disbursement date, approved before paid) with no figure moving: assert Dr Souter's position, the whole ledger, the GST schedule and S3's figures are identical before and after. Two seeded payouts (15 and 16 Jul) fall inside the current period, so live runs take the next sequence and id. Bump PERSIST_VERSION for the new slices, setting, leg field and backdrop runs, and extend the migrate and seed tests.
- Demo triggers go through the Phase 14 registry, shown only on their screen, with disabled states and bodies in src/store or src/shared: new "Stage a payment period" (session 1; choices "19 BCTIs" and "19 BCTIs and a credit after payout", service dates before the canvas horizon start, payments through the ordinary webhook) and "Stage departed anaesthetist" (session 2) on the Billing monitor, both credits built by one pure credit-after-payout fixture builder over 39's pure credit functions; re-point Phase 39's "Stage refund after payout" (payout through approval, result text), Phase 37's "Pay anaesthetist in Xero (payables run)", Phase 16's "Record fee payment" (for recovery invoices) and Phase 36's PWA "Office runs payables" stand-in. "Approve period's BCTIs", "Run payables in Xero", "Re-approve" and "Invoice carried-forward balances" are product buttons, not registry entries. Nothing is added to the Control Panel page. No new PWA entry is needed; confirm the re-pointed stand-in works in the PWA build and pwaPurity.test.ts stays green.
- Every new audit action gets an ACTION_LABELS entry and the right actor (office for approval, recovery and settings; system for detection; demo for the Xero-side batch payment).
- Follow the design files: teal is the only action colour, crimson stays identity only, pills use semantic tokens, amounts are mono with tabular-nums, negatives render "-$X.XX" through formatSignedCurrency (neutral, never red), provisional labels for OQ-47, OQ-71 and the recovery GST. 39b

Pre-op and post-op events

Capture After phases 23, NaN, 39 2 sessions plan doc ↗ 6 items covered

Anaesthetist adds pre/post-op events (time or fixed fee, billable or not) to a past or current Procedure on mobile and web. Office approves each billable one, and it gets its own one-line invoice traceable to the Procedure. ACC pre-op becomes a fixed-fee pre-op event (codes provisional). Billing lines gain their own date, preset types and Contract add-ons. A line added after submit takes the same approval path.

DM-17FT-03.7boardUS-03.7.1boardUS-03.7.2boardUS-05.5.2boardUS-03.3.6board

Open questions: OQ-12 · OQ-63

Demo triggers and risks
  • Mobile and web Procedure: 'Add pre-op or post-op event' (product), on a past billed Procedure of Dr Souter's that the re-pointed 'Stage post-op scenario' provides
  • Mobile and web Procedure: 'Add billing line' with a preset type or a Contract add-on fee, dated days after the procedure (product)
  • Admin event approvals: 'Approve' and 'Decline' (product)
  • PWA sheet, mobile Procedure: 'Office approves this event' (audited as the office; the invoice appears)

Risks. The model, name and approval of events are OQ-63 (Greg leans to adding a procedure, Donald to a separate element): keep the event a thin record with one store action per step, so a switch stays contained, and keep its user-facing name in one label. The ACC codes are OQ-12 (AA's accountant): label them provisional. Re-point 'Stage post-op scenario' to Dr Souter, the only anaesthetist persona. The fee stays hidden on the anaesthetist side. Mobile-first: bottom sheets, not desktop forms. Seed at least one Contract with add-on lines that Dr Souter's Procedures use. An event's own invoice is its own BCTI and feeds Phase 16's count function.

Parked

In scope, but deliberately not planned yet.

Milestones

What a workshop can be shown once each milestone phase is done.

  • After phase 16
    Booking vocabulary everywhere. Every demo action on its own screen, in the harness bar and on a handset, with the HL7/FHIR tooling badged Future scope. Warnings, never blocks (15a runs before 16): one routine, the to-do list, a triangle on Bookings, and the prepayment gate turned into a warning. The corrected payables story: the payable equals the receivable, and AA's fee is a monthly invoice built from its settings.
  • After phase 25
    The catalogue's pricing model end to end: editable RVG, modifier and master procedure lists feeding base units, with any value accepted and an office warning when out of range; a procedure-first pick, then one Contract per Procedure filtered by procedure and hospital, with holder-code search and a default; billable party defaulting from the Contract, required inputs and a child warning; invoices sent by the billing run, with a full or split payment setting; the 3/2/2 modifier split, combination Contracts and a Contract-gated adjustment; and a lock at AUTHORISED that reproduces invoices exactly. S3, S4 Beat 3 and S5 are re-scripted.
  • After phase 27
    Prepayment from the anaesthetist's prepaid set for a paying patient: the estimate from estimated duration, the invoice generated at setup and sent on admin approval, part-paid tracking, a warning in both apps that strengthens as the date nears, and the balance invoice. S4 Beat 1 is re-scripted.
  • After phase 32
    The office's schedule: Slots and Lists, the availability calendar with series, the conflict dashboard, Draft Lists that hold Bookings with a waiting flag (including from unavailability), blacklist warnings, and anaesthetists moving their own Lists to the office or a colleague. S2 is re-scripted.
  • After phase 35
    S1 rebuilt: hospital sync, then the matching screen, then a Booking on its default Contract. Explicit save and the on-demand update email to the rooms or the hospital.
  • After phase 39a
    The money story on the internal ledger: balances shown in or out of balance, Xero mirror resilience, ledger-backed web accounts with a flat outstanding list and a cash-basis GST schedule, free-form additional invoices (including a split combined procedure), credit-and-rebill, and payment runs with BCTI approval, netting, a remittance advice and recovery of a carried-forward negative. S4 Beats 2 to 5 are re-scripted.
  • After phase 44
    Every verified gap closed: finding past work, pre-op and post-op events, patients with balance warnings and NHI lookup, prepayment letters, the trust account and refunds, reference-data loads, full-scale and privacy demos, point-of-need help, and the rewritten S1 to S5 run sheet.
